3	  "idea": "# Sprint 1a \u2014 agent_kit core + invocation mode\n\nBuild the foundational Python package for Arnold, a Discord planning bot. This sprint proves the substrate against the cheapest possible adapter (CLI over SQLite) before Discord/Supabase land in Sprint 1b.\n\n**Full spec is at `planning-bot-spec.md` in this repo root. Refer to it for complete data model schemas, tool signatures, and architectural details.**\n\n## Supabase (for later sprints)\n- URL: https://yhwflvadmefhkshwbfnf.supabase.co\n- Service key: [REDACTED].[REDACTED].xFknK1AD9JF6JIcGTYYOyOLUQNNj_WMNGL08K9Xr2NY\n\n## Scope\n\n- `agent_kit/` package skeleton:\n  - `ports.py` \u2014 Transport, Store, Model, Blob protocol definitions (abstract interfaces)\n  - `loop.py` \u2014 transport-agnostic `run_turn` function\n  - `tool_kit.py` \u2014 tool registry + audit-wrap decorator\n  - `ledger.py` \u2014 event store skeleton\n- `Envelope` dataclass + JSON schema (outcome: completed/blocked_on_caller/errored/aborted, events array, state_delta, reply)\n- CLI transport adapter (pull mode): `arnold turn --epic <id> --input \"text\"` command; NDJSON event streaming with `--stream-events`; exit codes per envelope outcome (0=completed, 2=blocked, 1=errored, 3=aborted)\n- SQLite store adapter: schema for epics, messages, bot_turns, tool_calls, system_logs tables (resident-mode-only columns nullable); migrations under `agent_kit/store/migrations/sqlite/`\n- Anthropic model adapter: Claude Opus 4.7 wired to the loop\n- Centralized logger module writing to system_logs via the Store port\n- Agentic loop skeleton: receive \u2192 reason \u2192 respond, returns an envelope\n- Minimal tool surface: `send_message` (writes to reply buffer in invocation mode), `set_activity` (emits event)\n- Test harness: pytest setup, ephemeral SQLite per test, mocked Anthropic client\n\n## Key Data Model Tables (this sprint)\n\n### epics\nid, title, goal, body (markdown), state (shaping|sprinting|planned|paused|archived), created_at, last_edited_at, last_active_at, planned_at\n\n### messages\nid, epic_id, direction (inbound|outbound), content, sent_at, discord_message_id (unique), has_code_attachment, has_image_attachment, in_burst_with, was_voice_message, audio_storage_url, transcription_metadata\n\n### bot_turns\nid, epic_id, triggered_by_message_ids (uuid array), prompt_snapshot, prompt_version, reasoning, final_output_message_id, status_message_id, status (in_progress|completed|failed|abandoned), state_at_turn, started_at, completed_at, model_version\n\n### tool_calls\nid, turn_id, tool_name, operation_kind (read|write), arguments (json), result (json), called_at, duration_ms\n\n### system_logs\nid, level (debug|info|warn|error), category, event_type, message, details (json), turn_id, epic_id, occurred_at\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- `arnold turn --epic <id> --input \"hello\"` returns a valid envelope on stdout; exit code 0 for completed, 2 for blocked_on_caller, 1 for errored, 3 for aborted\n- Every Anthropic call recorded in bot_turns; every tool call recorded in tool_calls; envelope events array matches tool_calls rows\n- Same input + same store state + same model seed \u2192 identical state_delta (deterministic-structure test)\n- Python `run_turn(...)` produces a byte-equivalent envelope to the CLI for the same input\n- `--stream-events` emits NDJSON to stderr with one event per tool call; final envelope on stdout matches\n- Test suite runs green via pytest against ephemeral SQLite\n\n## Tests\n\n- Unit: envelope schema validation; Store port contract tests against SQLite impl; tool_kit audit-wrap atomicity\n- Integration: full receive\u2192reason\u2192respond loop with mocked Anthropic; verify envelope contents and DB state\n- Integration: CLI \u2194 Python equivalence \u2014 invoke same input both ways, assert envelopes match (modulo non-deterministic reply text)\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.11+\n- pytest for testing\n- anthropic SDK for Claude Opus 4.7\n- SQLite for local store\n- Click or argparse for CLI\n- dataclasses for data structures",

migrations under `agent_kit/store/migrations/sqlite/`\n- Anthropic model adapter: Claude Opus 4.7 wired to the loop\n- Centralized logger module writing to system_logs via the Store port\n- Agentic loop skeleton: receive \u2192 reason \u2192 respond, returns an envelope\n- Minimal tool surface: `send_message` (writes to reply buffer in invocation mode), `set_activity` (emits event)\n- Test harness: pytest setup, ephemeral SQLite per test, mocked Anthropic client\n\n## Key Data Model Tables (this sprint)\n\n### epics\nid, title, goal, body (markdown), state (shaping|sprinting|planned|paused|archived), created_at, last_edited_at, last_active_at, planned_at\n\n### messages\nid, epic_id, direction (inbound|outbound), content, sent_at, discord_message_id (unique), has_code_attachment, has_image_attachment, in_burst_with, was_voice_message, audio_storage_url, transcription_metadata\n\n### bot_turns\nid, epic_id, triggered_by_message_ids (uuid array), prompt_snapshot, prompt_version, reasoning, final_output_message_id, status_message_id, status (in_progress|completed|failed|abandoned), state_at_turn, started_at, completed_at, model_version\n\n### tool_calls\nid, turn_id, tool_name, operation_kind (read|write), arguments (json), result (json), called_at, duration_ms\n\n### system_logs\nid, level (debug|info|warn|error), category, event_type, message, details (json), turn_id, epic_id, occurred_at\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- `arnold turn --epic <id> --input \"hello\"` returns a valid envelope on stdout; exit code 0 for completed, 2 for blocked_on_caller, 1 for errored, 3 for aborted\n- Every Anthropic call recorded in bot_turns; every tool call recorded in tool_calls; envelope events array matches tool_calls rows\n- Same input + same store state + same model seed \u2192 identical state_delta (deterministic-structure test)\n- Python `run_turn(...)` produces a byte-equivalent envelope to the CLI for the same input\n- `--stream-events` emits NDJSON to stderr with one event per tool call; final envelope on stdout matches\n- Test suite runs green via pytest against ephemeral SQLite\n\n## Tests\n\n- Unit: envelope schema validation; Store port contract tests against SQLite impl; tool_kit audit-wrap atomicity\n- Integration: full receive\u2192reason\u2192respond loop with mocked Anthropic; 1	"""Auto-driver that advances a plan through its phases without human intervention.
2	
3	This is the mechanical loop that most orchestrators end up writing by hand:
4	read `status`, run `next_step`, repeat until terminal. All real judgment is
5	delegated to megaplan's existing phase logic — the driver only applies two
6	documented defaults:
7	
8	1. Gate ESCALATE → force-proceed (caller opts out with ``--on-escalate abort``
9	   or ``--on-escalate fail``).
10	2. Same state for N consecutive iterations → bail (stall detection).
11	
12	The driver is intentionally dumb. If a run needs judgment the driver can't
13	provide, it exits with a non-zero status and prints the state snapshot so the
14	caller can intervene.
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations

43	CONTEXT_EXHAUSTION_FRAGMENT = "ran out of room in the model's context"
44	# When execute exits 0 but state.json's latest execute entry is `result=blocked`,
45	# the executor reported success-with-evidence-gaps (e.g. done tasks missing
46	# files_changed/commands_run). Retrying the same execute is structurally pointless
47	# — the model returned that shape — so we cap retries low and fail fast.
48	DEFAULT_MAX_BLOCKED_RETRIES = 1
49	# Cap on review→rework cycles before the driver bails. This mirrors the
50	# `execution.max_review_rework_cycles` config the review handler enforces
51	# internally (default 3); the auto-driver applies its own cap so that an
52	# unexpected-config or mis-routed rework loop cannot spin indefinitely.
53	DEFAULT_MAX_REVIEW_REWORK_CYCLES = 3

120	    args: list[str],
121	    *,
122	    cwd: Path | None = None,
123	    timeout: float | None = None,
124	) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
125	    """Run a megaplan sub-command in its own process.
126	
127	    We shell out rather than importing the handlers directly so each phase gets
128	    a fresh argparse/handler lifecycle. This matches how external orchestrators
129	    drive the CLI and avoids subtle state leakage between phases.
130	
131	    ``timeout`` is seconds to wait before killing the subprocess. On timeout we
132	    return exit code ``PHASE_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODE`` and append a marker to stderr
133	    so the driver can surface it as a phase failure without crashing the loop.
134	    The subprocess is killed; any grandchildren it spawned (e.g. codex) may
135	    need a moment to settle but will exit when their parent's pipes close.
136	    """
137	    try:
138	        proc = subprocess.run(
139	            [sys.executable, "-m", "megaplan", *args],
140	            cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
141	            capture_output=True,
142	            text=True,
143	            check=False,
144	            timeout=timeout,
145	        )
146	        return proc.returncode, proc.stdout, proc.stderr
147	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as expired:
148	        stdout = expired.stdout if isinstance(expired.stdout, str) else ""
149	        stderr = expired.stderr if isinstance(expired.stderr, str) else ""
150	        marker = f"\n[megaplan auto] subprocess timed out after {timeout}s"
151	        return PHASE_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODE, stdout, (stderr + marker).strip()
152

154	def _status(
155	    plan: str,
156	    cwd: Path | None = None,
157	    *,
158	    timeout: float = DEFAULT_STATUS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
159	) -> dict[str, Any]:
160	    code, out, err = _run_megaplan(["status", "--plan", plan], cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout)
161	    if code != 0:
162	        raise RuntimeError(f"megaplan status failed (exit {code}): {err.strip() or out.strip()}")
163	    return json.loads(out)

166	def _has_valid_next(status: dict[str, Any], action: str) -> bool:
167	    return action in (status.get("valid_next") or [])
168	
169	
170	def _phase_command(next_step: str) -> list[str]:
171	    """Translate a `next_step` from status into the CLI args that run it.
172	
173	    Most phases are one-to-one: next_step == command. Execute adds the
174	    destructive + user-approved flags because auto-mode implies both.
175	    """
176	    if next_step == "execute":
177	        return ["execute", "--confirm-destructive", "--user-approved"]
178	    return [next_step]
179	
180	
181	def _resolve_plan_dir(plan: str, cwd: Path | None) -> Path | None:
182	    """Best-effort resolution of ``.megaplan/plans/<plan>`` near ``cwd``.
183	
184	    Walks up parents of ``cwd`` looking for a ``.megaplan/plans/<plan>``
185	    directory, matching how ``megaplan status`` resolves plans. Returns
186	    ``None`` if the plan dir can't be located — callers should treat that
187	    as "no review marker available" and fall back to the plain stall-count.
188	    """
189	    base = (cwd or Path.cwd()).resolve()
190	    for candidate in (base, *base.parents):
191	        plan_dir = candidate / ".megaplan" / "plans" / plan
192	        if (plan_dir / "state.json").exists():
193	            return plan_dir
194	    return None

106	    root: Path | None,
107	) -> None:
108	    plan_dir = _resolve_error_plan_dir(root, error)
109	    details = payload.get("details")
110	    if not isinstance(details, dict):

130	def error_response(error: CliError, *, root: Path | None = None) -> int:
131	    payload: StepResponse = {

138	    if error.extra:

140	    if error.code == "plan_locked":
141	        _augment_plan_locked_error(payload, error, root=root)
142	    return render_response(payload, exit_code=error.exit_code)

148	    try:
149	        return datetime.fromisoformat(timestamp.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
150	    except ValueError:
151	        return None
152	
153	
154	def _build_progress_payload(plan_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
155	    finalize_path = plan_dir / "finalize.json"
156	    if not finalize_path.exists():
157	        return {
158	            "summary": "No finalize.json yet — plan has not been finalized.",
159	            "tasks_total": 0,
160	            "tasks_done": 0,
161	            "tasks_skipped": 0,
162	            "tasks_pending": 0,
163	            "tasks_blocked": 0,
164	            "batches_total": 0,
165	            "batches_completed": 0,
166	            "tasks": [],
167	        }
168	    finalize_data = read_json(finalize_path)
169	    global_batches = compute_global_batches(finalize_data)
170	    tasks = finalize_data.get("tasks", [])
171	    task_id_to_batch: dict[str, int] = {}
172	    for batch_idx, batch_ids in enumerate(global_batches, start=1):
173	        for task_id in batch_ids:
174	            task_id_to_batch[task_id] = batch_idx
175	    tasks_done = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.get("status") == "done")
176	    tasks_skipped = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.get("status") == "skipped")
177	    tasks_pending = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.get("status") == "pending")
178	    tasks_blocked = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.get("status") == "blocked")
179	    tasks_total = len(tasks)
180	    completed_ids = {
181	        t["id"] for t in tasks if t.get("status") in {"done", "skipped"} and isinstance(t.get("id"), str)
182	    }
183	    batches_completed = sum(
184	        1
185	        for batch_ids in global_batches
186	        if all(tid in completed_ids for tid in batch_ids)
187	    )
188	    task_status_list = [
189	        {
190	            "id": t.get("id", ""),
191	            "status": t.get("status", "pending"),
192	            "batch": task_id_to_batch.get(t.get("id", ""), 0),
193	        }
194	        for t in tasks
195	    ]
196	    return {
197	        "summary": (
198	            f"Execution progress: {tasks_done + tasks_skipped}/{tasks_total} tasks tracked, "
199	            f"{batches_completed}/{len(global_batches)} batches completed. "
200	            "Progress reflects the last finalize.json write (between-batch granularity)."
201	        ),
202	        "tasks_total": tasks_total,
203	        "tasks_done": tasks_done,
204	        "tasks_skipped": tasks_skipped,
205	        "tasks_pending": tasks_pending,
206	        "tasks_blocked": tasks_blocked,
207	        "batches_total": len(global_batches),
208	        "batches_completed": batches_completed,
209	        "tasks": task_status_list,
210	    }
211	
212	
213	def _build_last_step(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
214	    history = state.get("history", [])
215	    if not isinstance(history, list) or not history:
216	        return None
217	    last = history[-1]
218	    if not isinstance(last, dict):
219	        return None
220	    return {
221	        "step": last.get("step"),
222	        "result": last.get("result"),
223	        "timestamp": last.get("timestamp"),
224	        "agent": last.get("agent"),
225	        "output_file": last.get("output_file"),
226	    }
227	
228	
229	def _build_active_step(active_step: Any, *, plan_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
230	    if not isinstance(active_step, dict):
231	        return None
232	    details = dict(active_step)
233	    step = details.get("step")
234	    if not isinstance(step, str) or not step:
235	        return details
236	    configured_timeout_seconds = int(get_effective("execution", "worker_timeout_seconds"))
237	    lock_held = plan_lock_is_held(plan_dir)
238	    started_at = _parse_utc_timestamp(details.get("started_at"))
239	    if started_at is not None:
240	        age_seconds = max(0, int((datetime.now(timezone.utc) - started_at).total_seconds()))
241	        details.update(
242	            build_phase_observability(
243	                step,
244	                configured_timeout_seconds=configured_timeout_seconds,
245	                age_seconds=age_seconds,
246	                lock_held=lock_held,
247	            )
248	        )
249	        if details.get("stale"):
250	            orphaned = not lock_held
251	            details["orphaned"] = orphaned
252	            if orphaned:
253	                if step == "execute":
254	                    details["recovery_hint"] = (
255	                        "The active step is stale and no process holds the plan lock. "
256	                        "Safe next action: rerun the same execute command on Codex without --fresh."
257	                    )
258	                else:
259	                    details["recovery_hint"] = (
260	                        "The active step is stale and no process holds the plan lock. "
261	                        "Safe next action: rerun the same step on the same agent before escalating."
262	                    )
263	        max_seconds = int(details.get("expected_duration_seconds", {}).get("max", 0) or 0)
264	        elapsed_label = humanize_seconds(age_seconds)
265	        if details.get("stale"):
266	            details["phase_progress_summary"] = (
267	                f"{step} stale ({elapsed_label} elapsed, expected max {humanize_seconds(max_seconds)}) "
268	                "see recovery_hint."
269	            )
270	        elif step in {"execute", "loop_execute"}:
271	            details["phase_progress_summary"] = (
272	                f"{step} running ({elapsed_label} elapsed, use progress for batch-level detail)."
273	            )
274	        else:
275	            details["phase_progress_summary"] = (
276	                f"{step} running ({elapsed_label} elapsed, typically completes within "
277	                f"{humanize_seconds(max_seconds)})."
278	            )
279	            if max_seconds > 0:
280	                details["progress_pct"] = min(95, int((age_seconds / max_seconds) * 100))
281	    else:
282	        details.update(
283	            build_phase_observability(
284	                step,
285	                configured_timeout_seconds=configured_timeout_seconds,
286	                lock_held=lock_held,
287	            )
288	        )
289	        if step in {"execute", "loop_execute"}:
290	            details["phase_progress_summary"] = (
291	                f"{step} active (start time unknown, use progress for batch-level detail)."
292	            )
293	        else:
294	            details["phase_progress_summary"] = f"{step} active (start time unknown)."
295	    return details
296	
297	
298	def _build_status_payload(plan_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> StepResponse:
299	    next_steps = infer_next_steps(state)
300	    notes = state.get("meta", {}).get("notes", [])
301	    lock_path = plan_dir / ".plan.lock"
302	    lock_file_present = lock_path.exists()
303	    lock_held = plan_lock_is_held(plan_dir)
304	    active_step = _build_active_step(state.get("active_step"), plan_dir=plan_dir)
305	    last_step = _build_last_step(state)
306	    plan_mode = state.get("config", {}).get("mode", "code")
307	    plan_output_path = state.get("config", {}).get("output_path")
308	    summary = f"Plan '{state['name']}' is currently in state '{state['current_state']}'."
309	    if is_prose_mode(state):
310	        summary += f" Mode: {plan_mode}. Output: {plan_output_path}."
311	    if active_step:
312	        summary = (
313	            summary
314	            + f" Active step: {active_step.get('step')} via {active_step.get('agent')}."
315	        )
316	    elif lock_file_present and not lock_held:
317	        summary = (
318	            summary
319	            + " No active step. The `.plan.lock` file may remain on disk even when no process holds the lock."
320	        )
321	    response: StepResponse = {
322	        "success": True,
323	        "step": "status",
324	        "plan": state["name"],
325	        "state": state["current_state"],
326	        "iteration": state["iteration"],
327	        "summary": summary,
328	        "next_step": next_steps[0] if next_steps else None,
329	        "valid_next": next_steps,
330	        "artifacts": sorted(
331	            path.name
332	            for path in plan_dir.iterdir()
333	            if path.is_file() and path.name != ".plan.lock"
334	        ),
335	        "lock_file_present": lock_file_present,
336	        "lock_held": lock_held,
337	        "active_step": active_step,
338	        "last_step": last_step,
339	        "total_cost_usd": state.get("meta", {}).get("total_cost_usd", 0.0),
340	        "mode": plan_mode,
341	        "output_path": plan_output_path,
342	        "notes_count": len(notes) if isinstance(notes, list) else 0,
343	        "notes": notes if isinstance(notes, list) else [],
344	        "session_summaries": [
345	            {"key": key, **value}
346	            for key, value in sorted(state.get("sessions", {}).items())
347	            if isinstance(value, dict)
348	        ],
349	    }
350	    runtime = build_next_step_runtime(
351	        response.get("next_step"),
352	        configured_timeout_seconds=int(get_effective("execution", "worker_timeout_seconds")),
353	    )
354	    if runtime is not None:
355	        response["next_step_runtime"] = runtime
356	    progress = _build_progress_payload(plan_dir, state) if (plan_dir / "finalize.json").exists() else None
357	    if progress is not None:
358	        response["progress"] = progress
359	        response["summary"] = response["summary"] + " " + progress["summary"]
360	    return response
361	
362	
363	def handle_status(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
364	    if getattr(args, "pending_human", False):
365	        items = []

367	            st = read_json(pd / "state.json")
368	            if st.get("current_state") == "awaiting_human_verify":
369	                items.append({"name": st["name"], "state": st["current_state"]})
370	        return {
371	            "success": True,
372	            "step": "status",
373	            "summary": f"Found {len(items)} plan(s) awaiting human verification.",
374	            "plans": items,
375	        }
376	    plan_dir, state = load_plan(root, args.plan)
377	    return _build_status_payload(plan_dir, state)

412	def _collect_megaplan_roots(root: Path, *, tree: bool = False, all_system: bool = False) -> list[Path]:
413	    """Collect .megaplan root directories based on search mode."""
414	    roots: list[Path] = [root]
415	
416	    if all_system:
417	        # Search from home directory downward for all .megaplan directories
418	        home = Path.home()
419	        for megaplan_dir in sorted(home.rglob(".megaplan")):
420	            if megaplan_dir.is_dir() and (megaplan_dir / "plans").is_dir():
421	                candidate = megaplan_dir.parent
422	                if candidate.resolve() != root.resolve():
423	                    roots.append(candidate)
424	    elif tree:
425	        # Walk up to find parent .megaplan directories
426	        current = root.resolve().parent
427	        while True:
428	            if (current / ".megaplan" / "plans").is_dir() and current.resolve() != root.resolve():
429	                roots.append(current)
430	            parent = current.parent
431	            if parent == current:
432	                break
433	            current = parent
434	        # Walk down to find child .megaplan directories
435	        for megaplan_dir in sorted(root.rglob(".megaplan")):
436	            if megaplan_dir.is_dir() and (megaplan_dir / "plans").is_dir():
437	                candidate = megaplan_dir.parent
438	                if candidate.resolve() != root.resolve():
439	                    roots.append(candidate)
440	
441	    return roots
442	
443	
444	def handle_list(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
445	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
446	    filter_status = getattr(args, "filter_status", None)
447	    no_tree = getattr(args, "no_tree", False)
448	    include_done = getattr(args, "include_done", False)
449	    show_summary = getattr(args, "summary", False)
450	    search_all = getattr(args, "all", False)
451	    # Default: tree=True (parent+child), active-only (exclude done/aborted)
452	    # --status overrides the active filter (explicit filter = show exactly that)
453	    search_tree = not no_tree and not search_all
454	    filter_active = not include_done and not filter_status
455	
456	    roots = _collect_megaplan_roots(root, tree=search_tree, all_system=search_all)
457	    total_scanned = 0
458	    allowed_states: set[str] | None = None
459	    if filter_status:
460	        allowed_states = {s.strip() for s in filter_status.split(",")}
461	
462	    items = []
463	    state_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
464	    resolved_root = root.resolve()
465	    for search_root in roots:
466	        resolved_search = search_root.resolve()
467	        is_local = resolved_search == resolved_root
468	        for plan_dir in active_plan_dirs(search_root):
469	            state = read_json(plan_dir / "state.json")
470	            current_state = state["current_state"]
471	            state_counts[current_state] = state_counts.get(current_state, 0) + 1
472	            total_scanned += 1
473	
474	            if filter_active and current_state in TERMINAL_STATES:
475	                continue
476	            if allowed_states and current_state not in allowed_states:
477	                continue
478	
479	            next_steps = infer_next_steps(state)
480	            entry = {
481	                "name": state["name"],
482	                "idea": state["idea"],
483	                "state": current_state,
484	                "iteration": state["iteration"],
485	                "next_step": next_steps[0] if next_steps else None,
486	            }
487	            if not is_local:
488	                try:
489	                    rel = resolved_search.relative_to(resolved_root)
490	                    entry["location"] = f"./{rel}"
491	                    entry["direction"] = "child"
492	                except ValueError:
493	                    try:
494	                        resolved_root.relative_to(resolved_search)
495	                        entry["location"] = os.path.relpath(resolved_search, resolved_root)
496	                        entry["direction"] = "parent"
497	                    except ValueError:
498	                        entry["location"] = str(resolved_search)
499	                        entry["direction"] = "external"
500	            items.append(entry)
501	
502	    summary_parts = [f"Found {len(items)} plans"]
503	    if len(roots) > 1:
504	        summary_parts.append(f"across {len(roots)} directories")
505	    if allowed_states:
506	        summary_parts.append(f"matching {','.join(sorted(allowed_states))}")
507	    if filter_active:
508	        summary_parts.append("(active only)")
509	
510	    result: StepResponse = {
511	        "success": True,
512	        "step": "list",
513	        "summary": f"{'. '.join(summary_parts)}.",
514	        "plans": items,
515	    }
516	    if show_summary:
517	        result["state_summary"] = dict(sorted(state_counts.items()))
518	
519	    # Hints for discovering more plans
520	    hidden_done = total_scanned - len(items) if filter_active else 0
521	    hints: list[str] = []
522	    if hidden_done > 0:
523	        hints.append(f"{hidden_done} completed plans hidden (use --include-done to show)")
524	    if not search_all:
525	        hints.append("Use --all to search all plans system-wide")
526	    if hints:
527	        result["hints"] = hints
528	
529	    return result
530	
531	
532	def handle_debt(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
533	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
534	    action = args.debt_action
535	    registry = load_debt_registry(root)
536	    default_plan_id = getattr(args, "plan", None) or "manual"
537	
538	    if action == "list":
539	        entries = registry["entries"] if args.all else [entry for entry in registry["entries"] if not entry["resolved"]]
540	        grouped: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
541	        for entry in entries:
542	            grouped.setdefault(entry["subsystem"], []).append(entry)
543	        escalated = {
544	            subsystem: total
545	            for subsystem, total, _entries in escalated_subsystems(registry)
546	        }
547	        by_subsystem = [
548	            {
549	                "subsystem": subsystem,
550	                "escalated": subsystem in escalated,
551	                "total_occurrences": subsystem_occurrence_total(entries_for_subsystem)
552	                if not args.all
553	                else sum(entry["occurrence_count"] for entry in entries_for_subsystem if not entry["resolved"]),
554	                "entries": entries_for_subsystem,
555	            }
556	            for subsystem, entries_for_subsystem in sorted(grouped.items())
557	        ]
558	        return {
559	            "success": True,
560	            "step": "debt",
561	            "action": "list",
562	            "summary": f"Found {len(entries)} debt entries across {len(by_subsystem)} subsystem groups.",
563	            "details": {
564	                "entries": entries,
565	                "by_subsystem": by_subsystem,
566	                "escalated_subsystems": [
567	                    {"subsystem": subsystem, "total_occurrences": total}
568	                    for subsystem, total in sorted(escalated.items())
569	                ],
570	            },
571	        }
572	
573	    if action == "add":
574	        flag_ids = [
575	            flag_id.strip()
576	            for flag_id in (args.flag_ids or "").split(",")
577	            if flag_id.strip()
578	        ]
579	        entry = add_or_increment_debt(
580	            registry,
581	            subsystem=args.subsystem,
582	            concern=args.concern,
583	            flag_ids=flag_ids,
584	            plan_id=default_plan_id,
585	        )
586	        save_debt_registry(root, registry)
587	        return {
588	            "success": True,
589	            "step": "debt",
590	            "action": "add",
591	            "summary": f"Tracked debt entry {entry['id']} for subsystem '{entry['subsystem']}'.",
592	            "details": {"entry": entry},
593	        }
594	
595	    if action == "resolve":
596	        entry = resolve_debt(registry, args.debt_id, default_plan_id)
597	        save_debt_registry(root, registry)
598	        return {
599	            "success": True,
600	            "step": "debt",
601	            "action": "resolve",
602	            "summary": f"Resolved debt entry {entry['id']}.",
603	            "details": {"entry": entry},
604	        }
605	
606	    raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown debt action: {action}")
607

613	def _canonical_instructions() -> str:
614	    return resources.files("megaplan").joinpath("data", "instructions.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
615	
616	
617	_SKILL_HEADER = """\
618	---
619	name: megaplan
620	description: AI agent harness for coordinating Claude and GPT to make and execute extremely robust plans.
621	---
622	
623	"""
624	
625	_CURSOR_HEADER = """\
626	---
627	description: Use megaplan for high-rigor planning on complex, high-risk, or multi-stage tasks.
628	alwaysApply: false
629	---
630	
631	"""
632	
633	
634	def bundled_agents_md() -> str:
635	    return _canonical_instructions()

638	def _subagent_appendix(filename: str) -> str:
639	    content = resources.files("megaplan").joinpath("data", filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
640	    content = content.replace(
641	        "{max_execute_no_progress}",
642	        str(get_effective("execution", "max_execute_no_progress")),
643	    )
644	    content = content.replace(
645	        "{max_review_rework_cycles}",
646	        str(get_effective("execution", "max_review_rework_cycles")),
647	    )
648	    return content
649	
650	
651	def _claude_subagent_appendix() -> str:
652	    return _subagent_appendix("claude_subagent_appendix.md")
653	
654	
655	def _codex_subagent_appendix() -> str:
656	    return _subagent_appendix("codex_subagent_appendix.md")
657	
658	
659	def bundled_global_file(name: str) -> str:
660	    content = _canonical_instructions()
661	    if name == "claude_skill.md":
662	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content + "\n\n" + _claude_subagent_appendix()
663	    if name == "codex_skill.md":
664	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content + "\n\n" + _codex_subagent_appendix()
665	    if name == "skill.md":
666	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content
667	    if name == "cursor_rule.mdc":
668	        return _CURSOR_HEADER + content
669	    return content
670	
671	
672	_GLOBAL_TARGETS = [
673	    {"agent": "claude", "detect": ".claude", "path": ".claude/skills/megaplan/SKILL.md", "data": "claude_skill.md"},
674	    {"agent": "codex", "detect": ".codex", "path": ".codex/skills/megaplan/SKILL.md", "data": "codex_skill.md"},
675	    {"agent": "cursor", "detect": ".cursor", "path": ".cursor/rules/megaplan.mdc", "data": "cursor_rule.mdc"},
676	]
677	
678	
679	def _install_owned_file(path: Path, content: str, *, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, bool | str]:
680	    existed = path.exists()
681	    if existed and not force:
682	        if path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == content:
683	            return {"path": str(path), "skipped": True, "existed": True}
684	    atomic_write_text(path, content)
685	    return {"path": str(path), "skipped": False, "existed": existed}
686	
687	
688	def handle_setup_global(force: bool = False, home: Path | None = None) -> StepResponse:
689	    if home is None:
690	        home = Path.home()
691	    installed: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
692	    detected_count = 0
693	    for target in _GLOBAL_TARGETS:
694	        agent_dir = home / target["detect"]
695	        if not agent_dir.is_dir():
696	            installed.append({"agent": target["agent"], "path": str(home / target["path"]), "skipped": True, "reason": "not installed"})
697	            continue
698	        detected_count += 1
699	        result = _install_owned_file(home / target["path"], bundled_global_file(target["data"]), force=force)
700	        result["agent"] = target["agent"]
701	        installed.append(result)
702	    if detected_count == 0:
703	        return {
704	            "success": False, "step": "setup", "mode": "global",
705	            "summary": "No supported agents detected. Create one of ~/.claude/, ~/.codex/, or ~/.cursor/ and re-run.",
706	            "installed": installed,
707	        }
708	    available = detect_available_agents()
709	    config_path = None
710	    routing = None
711	    if available:
712	        agents_config = {step: (default if default in available else available[0]) for step, default in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING.items()}
713	        config = load_config(home)
714	        config["agents"] = agents_config
715	        config_path = save_config(config, home)
716	        routing = agents_config
717	    lines = []
718	    for rec in installed:
719	        if rec.get("reason") == "not installed":
720	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: skipped (not installed)")
721	        elif rec["skipped"]:
722	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: up to date")
723	        else:
724	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: {'overwrote' if rec['existed'] else 'created'} {rec['path']}")
725	    result_data: dict[str, Any] = {"success": True, "step": "setup", "mode": "global", "summary": "Global setup complete:\n" + "\n".join(lines), "installed": installed}
726	    if config_path is not None:
727	        result_data["config_path"] = str(config_path)
728	        result_data["routing"] = routing
729	    return result_data
730	
731	
732	def handle_setup(args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
733	    local = args.local or args.target_dir
734	    if not local:
735	        return handle_setup_global(force=args.force)
736	    target_dir = Path(args.target_dir).resolve() if args.target_dir else Path.cwd()
737	    target = target_dir / "AGENTS.md"
738	    content = bundled_agents_md()
739	    if target.exists() and not args.force:
740	        existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
741	        if "megaplan" in existing.lower():
742	            return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"AGENTS.md already contains megaplan instructions at {target}", "skipped": True}
743	        atomic_write_text(target, existing + "\n\n" + content)
744	        return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"Appended megaplan instructions to existing {target}", "file": str(target)}
745	    atomic_write_text(target, content)
746	    return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"Created {target}", "file": str(target)}
747	
748	
749	def handle_config(args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
750	    action = args.config_action
751	    if action == "show":
752	        config = load_config()
753	        effective_routing = {step: config.get("agents", {}).get(step, default) for step, default in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING.items()}
754	        effective_settings = {
755	            dot_key: get_effective(section, setting)
756	            for dot_key in sorted(DEFAULTS)
757	            for section, setting in [dot_key.split(".", 1)]
758	        }
759	        return {
760	            "success": True,
761	            "step": "config",
762	            "action": "show",
763	            "config_path": str(config_dir() / "config.json"),
764	            "routing": effective_routing,
765	            "effective_settings": effective_settings,
766	            "raw_config": config,
767	        }
768	    if action == "set":
769	        key, value = args.key, args.value
770	        parts = key.split(".", 1)
771	        config = load_config()
772	        valid_keys = [
773	            *(f"agents.{step}" for step in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING),
774	            "orchestration.mode",
775	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_BOOL),
776	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_ENUM),
777	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_NUMERIC),
778	        ]
779	        if len(parts) != 2:
780	            raise CliError(
781	                "invalid_args",
782	                f"Unknown config key '{key}'. Valid keys: {', '.join(valid_keys)}",
783	            )
784	        section, setting = parts
785	        normalized_value = value.strip().lower()
786	        if section == "agents":
787	            if setting not in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING:
788	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown step '{setting}'. Valid steps: {', '.join(DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING)}")
789	            if value not in KNOWN_AGENTS:
790	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown agent '{value}'. Valid agents: {', '.join(KNOWN_AGENTS)}")
791	            config.setdefault("agents", {})[setting] = value
792	        elif key == "orchestration.mode":
793	            if value not in {"inline", "subagent"}:
794	                raise CliError("invalid_args", "orchestration.mode must be 'inline' or 'subagent'")
795	            config.setdefault("orchestration", {})["mode"] = value
796	        elif key in _SETTABLE_BOOL:
797	            if normalized_value in {"true", "1", "yes", "on"}:
798	                parsed_value = True
799	            elif normalized_value in {"false", "0", "no", "off"}:
800	                parsed_value = False
801	            else:
802	                raise CliError(
803	                    "invalid_args",
804	                    f"{key} must be one of: true, false, 1, 0, yes, no, on, off",
805	                )
806	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = parsed_value
807	        elif key in _SETTABLE_ENUM:
808	            allowed_values = _SETTABLE_ENUM[key]
809	            if value not in allowed_values:
810	                raise CliError(
811	                    "invalid_args",
812	                    f"{key} must be one of: {', '.join(allowed_values)}",
813	                )
814	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = value
815	        elif key in _SETTABLE_NUMERIC:
816	            try:
817	                parsed_value = int(value)
818	            except ValueError as exc:
819	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"{key} must be an integer, got '{value}'") from exc
820	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = parsed_value
821	        else:
822	            raise CliError(
823	                "invalid_args",
824	                f"Unknown config key '{key}'. Valid keys: {', '.join(valid_keys)}",
825	            )
826	        save_config(config)
827	        return {"success": True, "step": "config", "action": "set", "key": key, "value": config[section][setting]}
828	    if action == "profiles":
829	        project_dir = Path.cwd()
830	        profiles_action = args.profiles_action
831	        if profiles_action == "list":
832	            profiles = [
833	                {
834	                    "source": source_label,
835	                    "name": profile_name,
836	                    "phases": phase_map,
837	                }
838	                for source_label, profile_name, phase_map in load_profile_sources(project_dir=project_dir)
839	            ]
840	            return {
841	                "success": True,
842	                "step": "config",
843	                "action": "profiles",
844	                "profiles_action": "list",
845	                "project_dir": str(project_dir),
846	                "profiles": profiles,
847	            }
848	        if profiles_action == "show":
849	            profiles = load_profiles(project_dir=project_dir)
850	            resolved = resolve_profile(args.name, profiles)
851	            return {
852	                "success": True,
853	                "step": "config",
854	                "action": "profiles",
855	                "profiles_action": "show",
856	                "project_dir": str(project_dir),
857	                "name": args.name,
858	                "profile": resolved,
859	            }
860	        raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown profiles action: {profiles_action}")
861	    if action == "reset":
862	        path = config_dir() / "config.json"
863	        if path.exists():
864	            path.unlink()
865	        return {"success": True, "step": "config", "action": "reset", "summary": "Config file removed. Using defaults."}
866	    raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown config action: {action}")
867

870	# Parser and dispatch

873	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
874	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Megaplan orchestration CLI")
875	    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
876	
877	    setup_parser = subparsers.add_parser("setup", help="Install megaplan into agent configs (global by default)")
878	    setup_parser.add_argument("--local", action="store_true", help="Install AGENTS.md into a project instead of global agent configs")
879	    setup_parser.add_argument("--target-dir", help="Directory to install into (default: cwd, implies --local)")
880	    setup_parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="Overwrite existing files")
881	
882	    init_parser = subparsers.add_parser("init")
883	    init_parser.add_argument("--project-dir", required=True)
884	    init_parser.add_argument("--name")
885	    init_parser.add_argument("--auto-approve", action="store_true", default=None)
886	    init_parser.add_argument(
887	        "--strict-notes",
888	        action="store_true",
889	        default=None,
890	        help=(
891	            "Reject force-proceed while unabsorbed user notes exist; turn ESCALATE "
892	            "guidance into a hard human-required signal. Auto-on for --mode metaplan/doc."
893	        ),
894	    )
895	    init_parser.add_argument("--robustness", choices=list(ROBUSTNESS_LEVELS), default=None)
896	    init_parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["code", "doc", "metaplan", "joke", "creative"], default=None,
897	                             help="Deliverable type: 'code' (source changes), 'doc' / 'metaplan' "
898	                                  "(design/spec artifact — 'metaplan' is an alias for 'doc'), or "
899	                                  "'joke' (film scene script; requires --output), or "
900	                                  "'creative' (creative work; requires --form and --output). "
901	                                  "Defaults to 'code' unless the idea strongly suggests a design document, "
902	                                  "in which case --mode must be passed explicitly.")
903	    init_parser.add_argument("--form", choices=available_form_ids(), default=None,
904	                             help="Creative form to use with --mode creative.")
905	    init_parser.add_argument("--output", default=None,
906	                             help="Relative path where the prose artifact will be written. "
907	                                  "Required with --mode doc, --mode joke, or --mode creative; rejected with --mode code.")
908	    init_parser.add_argument(
909	        "--primary-criterion",
910	        default=None,
911	        help="Declare the creative-work primary criterion (for example: 'weirdest coherent'). "
912	             "Valid only with --mode joke or --mode creative.",
913	    )
914	    init_parser.add_argument("--from-doc", default=None,
915	                             help="Relative path to a prior doc-mode artifact whose ## Settled "
916	                                  "Decisions section should be imported. Valid with --mode "
917	                                  "code, --mode doc, --mode joke, or --mode creative.")
918	    init_parser.add_argument(
919	        "--idea-file",
920	        default=None,
921	        help="Read the idea text from a UTF-8 file instead of the positional CLI argument.",
922	    )
923	    init_parser.add_argument(
924	        "--auto-start",
925	        action="store_true",
926	        help="Immediately run the in-process auto driver after initializing the plan.",
927	    )
928	    init_parser.add_argument("--hermes", nargs="?", const="", default=None,
929	                             help="Use Hermes agent for all phases. Optional: specify default model")
930	    init_parser.add_argument("--phase-model", action="append", default=[],
931	                             help="Per-phase model override: --phase-model critique=hermes:openai/gpt-5")
932	    init_parser.add_argument("--profile", default=None,
933	                             help="Named preset from profiles.toml; see 'megaplan config profiles list'.")
934	    init_parser.add_argument("idea", nargs="?")
935	
936	    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser("list")
937	    list_parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true",
938	                             help="Search all .megaplan directories system-wide (~)")

940	                             help="Only show plans from the current directory (default includes parent + child)")

1196	def _resolve_project_root(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path:
1197	    """Pick the authoritative project root for handlers that take ``root``.
1198	
1199	    Precedence:
1200	
1201	    1. ``--project-dir`` (when set on *args*) wins. The CLI flag is a deliberate
1202	       caller override; honoring CWD-based discovery here lets a stray
1203	       ``.megaplan/`` in an ancestor directory hijack the run. That's how
1204	       parallel `megaplan init` invocations from sibling worktrees under
1205	       ``~/Documents/.megaplan-worktrees/<exp>/<profile>/`` collide on a
1206	       ``duplicate_plan`` error — the walk-up hits ``~/Documents/.megaplan/``
1207	       and they all try to write into the same plans dir. See
1208	       ``megaplan/bakeoff/orchestrator.py:_init_profile`` for the spawning side.
1209	    2. Otherwise fall back to ``_find_megaplan_root(Path.cwd())`` — the legacy
1210	       behavior that lets ``megaplan plan`` / ``status`` / etc. find the
1211	       enclosing project without an explicit flag.
1212	    """
1213	    project_dir = getattr(args, "project_dir", None)
1214	    if project_dir:
1215	        resolved = Path(project_dir).expanduser().resolve()
1216	        if not resolved.exists() or not resolved.is_dir():
1217	            raise CliError(
1218	                "invalid_project_dir",
1219	                f"--project-dir does not exist or is not a directory: {project_dir}",
1220	            )
1221	        return resolved
1222	    return _find_megaplan_root(Path.cwd())
1223	
1224	
1225	def _find_megaplan_root(start: Path) -> Path:
1226	    """Walk up from *start* to find the git-root directory containing ``.megaplan/``.
1227	
1228	    Strategy: find the git root first (like ``git rev-parse --show-toplevel``),
1229	    then check if it has a ``.megaplan/`` directory.  This avoids ambiguity when
1230	    nested subdirectories also have their own ``.megaplan/``.  Falls back to the
1231	    nearest ancestor with ``.megaplan/`` if not in a git repo, and finally to
1232	    *start* if nothing is found.
1233	    """
1234	    resolved = start.resolve()
1235	
1236	    # Try git root first — the canonical project root.
1237	    git_root = _find_git_root(resolved)
1238	    if git_root and (git_root / ".megaplan").is_dir():
1239	        return git_root
1240	
1241	    # Fallback: walk up to find nearest .megaplan
1242	    current = resolved
1243	    while True:
1244	        if (current / ".megaplan").is_dir():
1245	            return current
1246	        parent = current.parent
1247	        if parent == current:
1248	            return start
1249	        current = parent
1250	
1251	
1252	def _find_git_root(start: Path) -> Path | None:
1253	    """Walk up to find the directory containing ``.git``."""
1254	    current = start
1255	    while True:
1256	        if (current / ".git").exists():
1257	            return current
1258	        parent = current.parent
1259	        if parent == current:
1260	            return None
1261	        current = parent
1262	
1263	
1264	def _auto_sync_installed_skills() -> None:
1265	    try:
1266	        for target in _GLOBAL_TARGETS:
1267	            agent_dir = Path.home() / target["detect"]
1268	            if not agent_dir.is_dir():
1269	                continue
1270	            _install_owned_file(Path.home() / target["path"], bundled_global_file(target["data"]), force=False)
1271	    except Exception:
1272	        pass
1273	
1274	
1275	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
1276	    if argv is None:
1277	        argv = sys.argv[1:]
1278	    if argv and argv[0] == "cloud":
1279	        from megaplan.cloud.cli import _register_cloud_subcommands, run_cloud_cli
1280	
1281	        cloud_parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="megaplan cloud")
1282	        _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)
1283	        cloud_args = cloud_parser.parse_args(argv[1:])
1284	        root = _find_megaplan_root(Path.cwd())
1285	        ensure_runtime_layout(root)
1286	        try:
1287	            return run_cloud_cli(root, cloud_args)
1288	        except CliError as error:
1289	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1290	    if argv and argv[0] == "bakeoff":
1291	        from megaplan.bakeoff.cli import _register_bakeoff_subcommands, run_bakeoff_cli
1292	
1293	        bakeoff_parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="megaplan bakeoff")
1294	        _register_bakeoff_subcommands(bakeoff_parser)
1295	        bakeoff_args = bakeoff_parser.parse_args(argv[1:])
1296	        root = _find_megaplan_root(Path.cwd())
1297	        ensure_runtime_layout(root)
1298	        try:
1299	            return run_bakeoff_cli(root, bakeoff_args)
1300	        except CliError as error:
1301	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1302	
1303	    parser = build_parser()
1304	    args, remaining = parser.parse_known_args(argv)
1305	    if args.command != "setup":
1306	        _auto_sync_installed_skills()
1307	    try:
1308	        if args.command == "setup":
1309	            return render_response(handle_setup(args))
1310	        if args.command == "config":
1311	            return render_response(handle_config(args))
1312	    except CliError as error:
1313	        return error_response(error)
1314	
1315	    # Capture an explicit --work-dir override for subprocess workers
1316	    # (--add-dir / -C). When the flag is NOT passed, leave the override unset
1317	    # so :func:`resolve_work_dir` can default to the plan's stored project_dir
1318	    # (persisted at ``megaplan init``). Defaulting to CWD here silently
1319	    # sandboxes codex to whatever subdirectory the shell happened to be in,
1320	    # which breaks cross-subrepo writes — see resolve_work_dir for the
1321	    # precedence rules.
1322	    from megaplan.workers import set_work_dir_override
1323	    work_dir_override = getattr(args, "work_dir", None)
1324	    set_work_dir_override(Path(work_dir_override) if work_dir_override else None)
1325	
1326	    try:
1327	        root = _resolve_project_root(args)
1328	    except CliError as error:
1329	        return error_response(error)
1330	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
1331	
1332	    if args.command == "auto":
1333	        from megaplan.auto import run_auto
1334	        try:
1335	            return run_auto(root, args)
1336	        except CliError as error:
1337	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1338	
1339	    if args.command == "chain":
1340	        from megaplan.chain import run_chain_cli
1341	        try:
1342	            return run_chain_cli(root, args)
1343	        except CliError as error:
1344	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1345	
1346	    if args.command == "tiebreaker":
1347	        from megaplan.prompts.tiebreaker_orchestrator import run_tiebreaker_cli
1348	        try:
1349	            return run_tiebreaker_cli(root, args)
1350	        except CliError as error:
1351	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1352	
1353	    try:
1354	        handler = COMMAND_HANDLERS.get(args.command)
1355	        if handler is None:
1356	            raise CliError("invalid_command", f"Unknown command {args.command!r}")
1357	        if args.command == "override" and remaining:
1358	            if not args.note:
1359	                args.note = " ".join(remaining)
1360	            remaining = []
1361	        if remaining:
1362	            parser.error(f"unrecognized arguments: {' '.join(remaining)}")
1363	        if args.command == "override" and args.override_action == "add-note" and not args.note:
1364	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "override add-note requires a note")
1365	        if args.command == "override" and args.override_action == "set-robustness" and not args.robustness:
1366	            raise CliError("invalid_args", f"override set-robustness requires --robustness {'|'.join(ROBUSTNESS_LEVELS)}")
1367	        return render_response(handler(root, args))
1368	    except CliError as error:
1369	        return error_response(error, root=root)

1371

1373	    sys.exit(main())

28	def handle_init(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
29	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
30	    project_dir = Path(args.project_dir).expanduser().resolve()
31	    if not project_dir.exists() or not project_dir.is_dir():
32	        raise CliError("invalid_project_dir", f"Project directory does not exist: {project_dir}")
33	    apply_profile_expansion(args, project_dir)
34	    positional_idea = getattr(args, "idea", None)
35	    idea_file = getattr(args, "idea_file", None)
36	    if positional_idea and idea_file:
37	        raise CliError("invalid_args", "Pass either the positional idea or --idea-file, not both")
38	    if idea_file:
39	        idea_path = Path(idea_file).expanduser().resolve()
40	        try:
41	            idea_text = idea_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
42	        except OSError as exc:
43	            raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unable to read --idea-file {idea_path}: {exc}") from exc
44	        if not idea_text:
45	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "--idea-file must contain non-empty UTF-8 text")
46	    elif positional_idea:
47	        idea_text = positional_idea
48	    else:
49	        raise CliError("invalid_args", "Provide an idea argument or --idea-file <path>")
50	    explicit_mode = getattr(args, "mode", None)
51	    raw_output_path = getattr(args, "output", None)
52	    raw_primary_criterion = getattr(args, "primary_criterion", None)
53	    raw_form = getattr(args, "form", None)
54	    mode = explicit_mode or "code"
55	    if mode == "metaplan":
56	        mode = "doc"
57	    if raw_primary_criterion and mode not in {"joke", "creative"}:
58	        raise CliError("invalid_args", "--primary-criterion is only valid with --mode joke or --mode creative")
59	    if raw_form and mode != "creative":
60	        raise CliError("invalid_args", "--form is only valid with --mode creative")
61	    if mode == "creative":
62	        if not raw_form:
63	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "--form is required when --mode creative is selected")
64	        if raw_form not in available_form_ids():
65	            raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown creative form: {raw_form}")
66	    elif mode == "joke":
67	        raw_form = "joke"
68

74	        )
75	    normalized_output_path: str | None = None

94	        imported_decisions, parse_warnings = extract_settled_decisions(

7	from typing import Any

10	from ..types import CliError, DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING, KNOWN_AGENTS, parse_agent_spec
11	
12	VALID_PHASE_KEYS = frozenset(DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING.keys())
13	
14	
15	def _known_profiles_text(profiles: dict[str, dict[str, str]]) -> str:
16	    names = sorted(profiles)
17	    return ", ".join(names) if names else "(none)"
18	
19	
20	def _raise_invalid_profile(path: Any, profile_name: str, key: str, message: str) -> None:
21	    raise CliError(
22	        "invalid_profile",
23	        f"Invalid profile '{profile_name}' in {path}: {message} (key: {key})",
24	    )
25	
26	
27	def _validate_profile_map(path: Any, profile_name: str, raw_profile: Any) -> dict[str, str]:
28	    if not isinstance(raw_profile, dict):
29	        raise CliError(
30	            "invalid_profile",
31	            f"Invalid profile '{profile_name}' in {path}: expected a TOML table of phase keys",
32	        )
33	    validated: dict[str, str] = {}
34	    for phase, raw_spec in raw_profile.items():
35	        if phase not in VALID_PHASE_KEYS:
36	            _raise_invalid_profile(
37	                path,
38	                profile_name,
39	                str(phase),
40	                f"unknown phase '{phase}'. Valid phases: {', '.join(sorted(VALID_PHASE_KEYS))}",
41	            )
42	        if not isinstance(raw_spec, str):
43	            _raise_invalid_profile(path, profile_name, phase, f"expected a string agent spec, got {type(raw_spec).__name__}")
44	        agent, _model = parse_agent_spec(raw_spec)
45	        if agent not in KNOWN_AGENTS:
46	            _raise_invalid_profile(
47	                path,
48	                profile_name,
49	                phase,
50	                f"unknown agent '{agent}' in spec {raw_spec!r}. Valid agents: {', '.join(KNOWN_AGENTS)}",
51	            )
52	        validated[str(phase)] = raw_spec
53	    return validated
54	
55	
56	def _parse_profiles_doc(path: Any, content: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
57	    try:
58	        data = tomllib.loads(content)
59	    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
60	        raise CliError("invalid_profile", f"Malformed TOML in {path}: {exc}") from exc
61	    if not data:
62	        return {}
63	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
64	        raise CliError("invalid_profile", f"Invalid profile file {path}: expected a TOML object at the top level")
65	    raw_profiles = data.get("profiles", {})
66	    if raw_profiles in ({}, None):
67	        return {}
68	    if not isinstance(raw_profiles, dict):
69	        raise CliError("invalid_profile", f"Invalid profile file {path}: [profiles] must be a TOML table")
70	    profiles: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}
71	    for profile_name, raw_profile in raw_profiles.items():
72	        profiles[profile_name] = _validate_profile_map(path, profile_name, raw_profile)
73	    return profiles
74	
75	
76	def _load_profiles_file(path: Any) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
77	    try:
78	        content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
79	    except FileNotFoundError:
80	        return {}
81	    except OSError as exc:
82	        raise CliError("invalid_profile", f"Unable to read profile file {path}: {exc}") from exc
83	    return _parse_profiles_doc(path, content)
84	
85	
86	def _built_in_profile_files() -> list[Any]:
87	    return sorted(
88	        (
89	            entry
90	            for entry in files("megaplan.profiles").iterdir()
91	            if entry.is_file() and entry.name.endswith(".toml")
92	        ),
93	        key=lambda entry: entry.name,
94	    )
95	
96	
97	def load_profile_sources(
98	    home: Path | None = None,
99	    project_dir: Path | None = None,
100	) -> list[tuple[str, str, dict[str, str]]]:
101	    sources: list[tuple[str, str, dict[str, str]]] = []
102	
103	    for path in _built_in_profile_files():
104	        for profile_name, phase_map in _load_profiles_file(path).items():
105	            sources.append(("built-in", profile_name, dict(phase_map)))
106	
107	    user_path = config_dir(home) / "profiles.toml"
108	    for profile_name, phase_map in _load_profiles_file(user_path).items():
109	        sources.append(("user", profile_name, dict(phase_map)))
110	
111	    if project_dir is not None:
112	        project_path = Path(project_dir) / ".megaplan" / "profiles.toml"
113	        for profile_name, phase_map in _load_profiles_file(project_path).items():
114	            sources.append(("project", profile_name, dict(phase_map)))
115	
116	    return sources
117	
118	
119	def load_profiles(
120	    home: Path | None = None,
121	    project_dir: Path | None = None,
122	) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
123	    profiles: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {}
124	    for _source_label, profile_name, phase_map in load_profile_sources(home=home, project_dir=project_dir):
125	        profiles[profile_name] = dict(phase_map)
126	    return profiles
127	
128	
129	def resolve_profile(name: str, profiles: dict[str, dict[str, str]]) -> dict[str, str]:
130	    try:
131	        return dict(profiles[name])
132	    except KeyError as exc:
133	        raise CliError(
134	            "unknown_profile",
135	            f"Unknown profile '{name}'. Known profiles: {_known_profiles_text(profiles)}",
136	        ) from exc
137	
138	
139	def profile_to_phase_models(profile: dict[str, str]) -> list[str]:
140	    return [f"{phase}={spec}" for phase, spec in profile.items()]
141	
142	
143	def apply_profile_expansion(
144	    args: argparse.Namespace,
145	    project_dir: Path | None,
146	    state: dict | None = None,
147	) -> argparse.Namespace:
148	    if getattr(args, "_profile_applied", False):
149	        return args
150	
151	    profile_name = getattr(args, "profile", None)
152	    if profile_name is None and state is not None:
153	        profile_name = (state.get("config") or {}).get("profile")
154	    phase_models = list(getattr(args, "phase_model", None) or [])
155	
156	    if profile_name:
157	        profiles = load_profiles(project_dir=project_dir)
158	        resolved = resolve_profile(profile_name, profiles)
159	        phase_models.extend(profile_to_phase_models(resolved))
160	        args.profile = profile_name
161	
162	    # Merge persisted --phase-model overrides from plan state. CLI flags on the
163	    # current step invocation take precedence; persisted values fill in gaps for
164	    # steps not specified on the CLI.
165	    if state is not None:
166	        persisted = list((state.get("config") or {}).get("phase_model") or [])
167	        cli_steps = {pm.split("=", 1)[0] for pm in phase_models if "=" in pm}
168	        for pm in persisted:
169	            if "=" in pm and pm.split("=", 1)[0] not in cli_steps:
170	                phase_models.append(pm)
171	
172	    args.phase_model = phase_models
173	    args._profile_applied = True
174	    return args
175	
176	
177	__all__ = [
178	    "VALID_PHASE_KEYS",
179	    "apply_profile_expansion",
180	    "load_profile_sources",
181	    "load_profiles",
182	    "profile_to_phase_models",
183	    "resolve_profile",
184	]

55	def _merge_imported_decision_criteria(
56	    state: PlanState,
57	    criteria: list[dict[str, Any]],
58	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
59	    imported_decisions = state["meta"].get("imported_decisions", [])
60	    if not imported_decisions:
61	        return criteria
62	
63	    merged = list(criteria)
64	    referenced_ids = {
65	        decision_id
66	        for decision in imported_decisions
67	        for decision_id in [decision.get("id")]
68	        if isinstance(decision_id, str)
69	        and decision_id
70	        and any(
71	            decision_id in criterion_text
72	            for criterion in merged
73	            for criterion_text in [criterion.get("criterion")]
74	            if isinstance(criterion, dict) and isinstance(criterion_text, str)
75	        )
76	    }
77	    for decision in imported_decisions:
78	        decision_id = decision.get("id")
79	        if not isinstance(decision_id, str) or not decision_id or decision_id in referenced_ids:
80	            continue
81	        decision_text = decision.get("decision", "")
82	        if not isinstance(decision_text, str):
83	            decision_text = str(decision_text)
84	        load_bearing = bool(decision.get("load_bearing"))
85	        merged.append(
86	            {
87	                "criterion": f"Plan adheres to imported decision {decision_id}: {decision_text}",
88	                "priority": "must" if load_bearing else "info",
89	                "requires": ["subjective_judgment"] if load_bearing else [],
90	            }
91	        )
92	        referenced_ids.add(decision_id)
93	    return merged

96	def _validate_relative_path(project_dir: Path, raw: str, flag_name: str) -> str:
97	    candidate = Path(raw)
98	    if candidate.is_absolute():
99	        raise CliError(
100	            "invalid_args",
101	            f"{flag_name} must be a relative path inside the project directory",
102	        )
103	    if any(part == ".." for part in candidate.parts):
104	        raise CliError("invalid_args", f"{flag_name} must not contain '..' path traversal")
105	    resolved_path = (project_dir / candidate).resolve()
106	    try:
107	        return resolved_path.relative_to(project_dir).as_posix()
108	    except ValueError as exc:
109	        raise CliError(
110	            "invalid_args",
111	            f"{flag_name} must stay within the project directory",
112	        ) from exc

142	def _run_worker(
143	    step: str,
144	    state: PlanState,
145	    plan_dir: Path,
146	    args: argparse.Namespace,
147	    *,
148	    root: Path,
149	    iteration: int | None = None,
150	    resolved: tuple[str, str, bool, str | None] | None = None,
151	    prompt_override: str | None = None,
152	    prompt_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
153	) -> tuple[WorkerResult, str, str, bool]:
154	    failure_iteration = state["iteration"] if iteration is None else iteration
155	    from megaplan import handlers as _handlers_pkg
156	
157	    apply_profile_expansion(args, Path(state["config"]["project_dir"]), state=state)
158	    agent, mode, refreshed, model = resolved or _handlers_pkg.resolve_agent_mode(step, args)
159	    run_id = set_active_step(state, step=step, agent=agent, mode=mode, model=model)
160	    _emit_phase_notice(step)
161	    # Phases hold the lock for many minutes; merge meta to avoid clobbering
162	    # concurrent override appends to ``meta.notes`` / ``meta.overrides``.
163	    save_state_merge_meta(plan_dir, state)
164	    try:
165	        run_step_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
166	            "root": root,
167	            "resolved": (agent, mode, refreshed, model),
168	            "prompt_override": prompt_override,
169	        }
170	        if prompt_kwargs is not None and _supports_prompt_kwargs(worker_module.run_step_with_worker):
171	            run_step_kwargs["prompt_kwargs"] = prompt_kwargs
172	        return worker_module.run_step_with_worker(
173	            step,
174	            state,
175	            plan_dir,
176	            args,
177	            **run_step_kwargs,
178	        )
179	    except CliError as error:
180	        clear_active_step(state, run_id=run_id)
181	        record_step_failure(plan_dir, state, step=step, iteration=failure_iteration, error=error)
182	        raise
183	    except Exception:
184	        clear_active_step(state, run_id=run_id)
185	        save_state_merge_meta(plan_dir, state)
186	        raise

189	def _supports_prompt_kwargs(run_step: Callable[..., Any]) -> bool:
190	    params = inspect.signature(run_step).parameters.values()
191	    return any(param.name == "prompt_kwargs" for param in params) or any(
192	        param.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for param in params

196	def _build_gate_prompt_override(
197	    agent_type: str,
198	    state: PlanState,
199	    plan_dir: Path,
200	    *,

202	    missing_flag_ids: list[str],

322	def _write_plan_version(
323	    *,
324	    plan_dir: Path,
325	    state: PlanState,
326	    step: str,
327	    version: int,
328	    worker: WorkerResult,
329	    plan_text: str,

333	    resolved_plan_filename = plan_filename or next_plan_artifact_name(plan_dir, version)
334	    meta_filename = (
335	        f"plan_v{version}.meta.json"
336	        if resolved_plan_filename == f"plan_v{version}.md"
337	        else resolved_plan_filename.replace(".md", ".meta.json")
338	    )
339	    structure_warnings = _validate_generated_plan_or_raise(
340	        plan_dir=plan_dir,
341	        state=state,
342	        step=step,
343	        iteration=version,
344	        worker=worker,

348	    meta = {

350	        "timestamp": now_utc(),
351	        "hash": sha256_text(plan_text),
352	        **meta_fields,

354	    }
355	    atomic_write_json(plan_dir / meta_filename, meta)
356	    return resolved_plan_filename, meta_filename, meta

359	def _validate_generated_plan_or_raise(
360	    *,
361	    plan_dir: Path,
362	    state: PlanState,
363	    step: str,
364	    iteration: int,
365	    worker: WorkerResult,
366	    plan_text: str,
367	) -> list[str]:
368	    structure_warnings = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
369	    if PLAN_STRUCTURE_REQUIRED_STEP_ISSUE in structure_warnings:
370	        error = CliError(
371	            "structure_error",
372	            f"{step.title()} output failed structural validation: {PLAN_STRUCTURE_REQUIRED_STEP_ISSUE}",
373	            valid_next=infer_next_steps(state),
374	            extra={"raw_output": worker.raw_output},
375	        )
376	        record_step_failure(
377	            plan_dir,
378	            state,
379	            step=step,
380	            iteration=iteration,
381	            error=error,
382	            duration_ms=worker.duration_ms,
383	        )
384	        raise error
385	    return structure_warnings

106	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
107	# Worker working directory resolution (git-worktree isolation)
108	#
109	# When megaplan is invoked from a git worktree, the plan's stored project_dir
110	# may point at a *different* checkout (usually the main repo). To avoid
111	# subprocess workers writing source code into the wrong working tree, resolve
112	# the "working directory" at CLI entry (CWD, or an explicit --work-dir
113	# override) and pass *that* through to the worker's --add-dir / -C flags.
114	#
115	# Plan state files (.megaplan/plans/...) still live under project_dir; only
116	# the source-code working tree tracked by the subprocess changes.
117	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
118	
119	_WORK_DIR_OVERRIDE: Path | None = None
120	_WORK_DIR_WARNED: bool = False
121	
122	
123	def set_work_dir_override(path: Path | str | None) -> None:
124	    """Set an explicit working directory for subprocess workers.
125	
126	    Typically called once from the CLI entry point with either an explicit
127	    --work-dir value or ``Path.cwd()``. Pass ``None`` to clear the override
128	    (primarily useful in tests).
129	    """
130	    global _WORK_DIR_OVERRIDE, _WORK_DIR_WARNED
131	    _WORK_DIR_OVERRIDE = Path(path) if path is not None else None
132	    _WORK_DIR_WARNED = False
133	
134	
135	def resolve_work_dir(state: PlanState) -> Path:
136	    """Return the source-code working directory for worker subprocesses.
137	
138	    Precedence:
139	    1. Explicit override set via :func:`set_work_dir_override` (e.g. from the
140	       CLI ``--work-dir`` flag).
141	    2. The plan's stored ``project_dir`` (persisted at ``megaplan init``).
142	    3. Current working directory (``Path.cwd()``) as a last-resort fallback
143	       when the plan has no ``project_dir`` recorded.
144	
145	    If the resolved path differs from the plan's stored ``project_dir``, a
146	    one-time informational line is printed so operators notice worktree
147	    divergence. (Callers that want a visually-loud operator warning should
148	    invoke :func:`warn_if_work_dir_differs_from_project_dir` from the phase
149	    entry point — this function keeps the log terse because it fires on every
150	    worker invocation.)
151	    """
152	    global _WORK_DIR_WARNED
153	    try:
154	        project_dir = Path(state["config"]["project_dir"]).resolve()
155	    except Exception:
156	        project_dir = None
157	    if _WORK_DIR_OVERRIDE is not None:
158	        work_dir = _WORK_DIR_OVERRIDE
159	    elif project_dir is not None:
160	        work_dir = project_dir
161	    else:
162	        work_dir = Path.cwd()
163	    try:
164	        resolved_work = work_dir.resolve()
165	    except Exception:
166	        resolved_work = work_dir
167	    if (
168	        not _WORK_DIR_WARNED
169	        and project_dir is not None
170	        and resolved_work != project_dir
171	    ):
172	        print(
173	            f"[megaplan] Using plan's project_dir ({project_dir}) for "
174	            f"subprocess --add-dir. Override with --work-dir if needed.",
175	            flush=True,
176	        )
177	        _WORK_DIR_WARNED = True
178	    return work_dir
179	
180	
181	def warn_if_work_dir_differs_from_project_dir(state: PlanState) -> None:
182	    """Emit a visible WARNING if the resolved work_dir is narrower than the
183	    plan's stored ``project_dir``.
184	
185	    Intended to be called at the top of any phase that spawns sandboxed
186	    subprocess workers (execute, review, etc.). The warning alerts the
187	    operator that codex will be sandboxed to a subset of the project tree,
188	    which silently breaks writes to sibling subrepos.
189	    """
190	    try:
191	        project_dir = Path(state["config"]["project_dir"]).resolve()
192	    except Exception:
193	        return
194	    work_dir = resolve_work_dir(state)
195	    try:
196	        resolved_work = work_dir.resolve()
197	    except Exception:
198	        resolved_work = work_dir
199	    if resolved_work == project_dir:
200	        return

222	def run_command(
223	    command: list[str],
224	    *,
225	    cwd: Path,
226	    stdin_text: str | None = None,
227	    env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
228	    timeout: int | None = None,
229	) -> CommandResult:
230	    started = time.monotonic()
231	    timeout = timeout or get_effective("execution", "worker_timeout_seconds")
232	    try:
233	        process = subprocess.run(
234	            command,
235	            cwd=str(cwd),
236	            input=stdin_text,
237	            text=True,
238	            capture_output=True,
239	            env=env,
240	            timeout=timeout,
241	        )
242	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
243	        raise CliError(
244	            "agent_not_found",
245	            f"Command not found: {command[0]}",
246	        ) from exc
247	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
248	        def _coerce_timeout_output(value: Any) -> str:
249	            if value is None:
250	                return ""
251	            if isinstance(value, bytes):
252	                return value.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
253	            return str(value)
254	
255	        raise CliError(
256	            "worker_timeout",
257	            f"Command timed out after {timeout}s: {' '.join(command[:3])}...",
258	            extra={"raw_output": _coerce_timeout_output(exc.stdout) + _coerce_timeout_output(exc.stderr)},
259	        ) from exc
260	    return CommandResult(
261	        command=command,
262	        cwd=cwd,
263	        returncode=process.returncode,
264	        stdout=process.stdout,
265	        stderr=process.stderr,
266	        duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
267	    )

300	def _codex_retry_guidance(step: str | None = None) -> str:
301	    if step in _EXECUTE_STEPS:
302	        return (
303	            "Re-run the same execute step on Codex once before changing agent; "
304	            "preserve the existing session path unless a fresh retry is explicitly needed."
305	        )
306	    return "Re-run the same step on Codex once before changing agent."
307	
308	
309	def _diagnose_codex_failure(raw: str, returncode: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
310	    """Parse Codex stderr/stdout for known error patterns. Returns (error_code, message)."""
311	    lower = raw.lower()
312	    for pattern, code, message in _CODEX_ERROR_PATTERNS:
313	        if pattern in lower:
314	            return code, f"{message}. {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
315	    if re.search(r"\bhttp\s*429\b", lower) or re.search(r"\b429\b", lower):
316	        return "rate_limit", f"Codex hit a rate limit (HTTP 429). {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
317	    if re.search(r"\bhttp\s*400\b", lower) or re.search(r"\b400\b", lower):
318	        return "schema_error", f"Codex API rejected request (HTTP 400). {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
319	    if re.search(r"\bhttp\s*500\b", lower) or re.search(r"\b500\b", lower):
320	        return "api_error", f"Codex API returned an internal error (HTTP 500). {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
321	    if re.search(r"\bhttp\s*502\b", lower) or re.search(r"\b502\b", lower):
322	        return "api_error", f"Codex API returned a gateway error (HTTP 502). {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
323	    if re.search(r"\bhttp\s*503\b", lower) or re.search(r"\b503\b", lower):
324	        return "api_error", f"Codex API service unavailable (HTTP 503). {_codex_retry_guidance()}"
325	    return "worker_error", (
326	        f"Codex step failed with exit code {returncode} (no recognized error pattern in output). "
327	        + _codex_retry_guidance()
328	    )
329

333	    return phase_timeout_seconds(step, configured_timeout_seconds=configured_timeout)
